The live alternative is a purely spatial reading of \"breasts.\"","changed_reading":{"before":"The Lord provides an outer shelter from an approaching threat.","after":"The Lord's refuge reaches the upstream source of conduct, repairing suggestion before it can issue as action."}},{"model_id":"delta_visible_hidden_social_field","status":"revised","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["ج ن ن","ن و س"],"mechanism":"The final contrast between concealed beings and visible people destabilizes a simple outside-enemy model. The people are both the collective under lordship and one possible source class, so refuge operates within familiar society against influence that may arrive through either hidden or human carriers.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ن و س","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"Visible human presence defines the focus collective and one side of the later visible-hidden contrast."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ن و س","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B003","role":"Familiar comfort supplies the social nearness that the final source list makes morally non-guaranteeing."},{"source_ref":"114:6","root":"ج ن ن","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Covering and concealment supply the hidden pole of the source field."},{"source_ref":"114:6","root":"ج ن ن","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000266","branch_id":"B005","role":"Concealed beings instantiate that hidden pole without exhausting the broader concealment mechanism."},{"source_ref":"114:6","root":"ن و س","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"Visible humans appear again as a possible source class, folding risk back into the protected collective."}],"structural_cues":["The final coordinated pair names concealed beings and people as sources, while \"the people\" has already been repeated as the object of all three divine titles.","The same noun therefore occupies both the protected domain and one side of the source domain."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies a visible-hidden contrast and places people on both sides of the protection relation; I infer that hidden-like influence can be socially carried. This does not imply that every familiar person is hostile; a live alternative is a simple two-species source list.","changed_reading":{"before":"The Lord of humanity protects people from an alien hidden enemy.","after":"The Lord of visible, familiar humanity protects within that same social field from influence carried by concealed agents or by people themselves."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"outlier_trembling_lift","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it depends on both non-dominant split mappings rather than the ordinary roots of \"say\" and \"Lord.\" It remains anchored to the exact focus words and yields a coherent motion from fear-tremor through bearing and rising into nurture; downstream prose should present it as a phonolexical activation, never as the lexical translation.","focus_anchor":"The command \"Say\" and the title \"Lord\" each carry a packet-supplied non-dominant mapped inventory that can couple bodily instability to upward formative care.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001251","branch_id":"B005","role":"A fear- or anger-shudder supplies the bodily instability from which commanded speech may begin."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001251","branch_id":"B004","role":"Carrying, lifting, and rising turn speech from symptom into a load-bearing upward movement."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ع و ذ","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001059","branch_id":"B001","role":"Protective turning gives the upward movement its direction and purpose."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000537","branch_id":"B001","role":"Increase and rising receive the lifted speaker into an enlarging rather than merely enclosed refuge."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000537","branch_id":"B005","role":"Nourishing growth makes the destination formative care rather than bare elevation."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"A stable speaker calmly recites a protective sentence.","after":"Even trembling speech can become the act that bears and lifts the vulnerable speaker toward enlarging, nutritive care."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_refusal_of_bargain","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This cross-domain reading is surprising because it uses the non-dominant exchange branch mapped from the context root for evil together with speech-negotiation and covenant. It remains anchored in \"Say, I seek refuge with the Lord\" and the later whisper, but should be rendered only as an analogy of solicited consent, not as a claim that \"evil\" means sale.","focus_anchor":"The focus combines voiced speech, protective transfer, and a Lord whose branch field includes binding covenant.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001272","branch_id":"B009","role":"Mutual verbal negotiation supplies the possibility that inner speech solicits agreement rather than simply attacks."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B011","role":"Covenant and protection agreement supply the competing bond affirmed by the refuge utterance."},{"source_ref":"114:4","root":"ش ر ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000792","branch_id":"B001","role":"Exchange between buying and selling supplies the exploratory transaction frame for harmful suggestion."},{"source_ref":"114:4","root":"و س و س","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001651","branch_id":"B001","role":"Hidden self-talk locates the proposed transaction inside deliberation, where consent can be withheld."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"The phrase asks to be shielded from an imposed evil.","after":"The phrase can also function as a voiced refusal to negotiate with harmful inner solicitation and a reaffirmation of the protector's covenant."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_social_spark_circulation","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it couples circulating public talk with fire-sparks and a faint whisper across social and material domains. It remains anchored in the focus's \"Say\" and \"people\" and in later branch-backed cues; downstream prose should mark it as a propagation analogy, not identify speech literally with fire.","focus_anchor":"The focus places one utterance inside the human collective, while its speech inventory includes talk that circulates among people.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001272","branch_id":"B007","role":"Circulating talk supplies the social transmission channel into which the commanded utterance enters."},{"source_ref":"114:1","root":"ن و س","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"Visible human presence supplies the network across which speech can pass."},{"source_ref":"114:4","root":"ش ر ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000787","branch_id":"B003","role":"Flying sparks supply the material analogy for many small transmissions escaping one source."},{"source_ref":"114:4","root":"و س و س","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001651","branch_id":"B002","role":"A faint voice shows that socially consequential transmission need not begin loudly."},{"source_ref":"114:6","root":"ن و س","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000059","branch_id":"B001","role":"People named as a source class close the loop from protected audience to possible human transmitter."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"The opening is a private individual's protective recitation.","after":"The opening is also a deliberate intervention in a human speech ecology where low-volume harmful talk can propagate in spark-like increments."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The opening utterance became an enacted attachment to formative care rather than a report that refuge is desired.","The title sequence revised refuge into voiced re-allegiance to one source of care, rule, and worship.","The hidden, recurrent whisper turned \"Say\" into an overt countervoice and \"I seek refuge\" into renewable reattachment.","The breast as an origin of action moved protection upstream into the formation of conduct.","The final visible-hidden pair placed human society on both sides of the protection relation."],"what_was_special":"The focus roots supported several coexisting mechanisms before context arrived: performative speech, caregiver attachment, inner-to-outer selection, and human-facing familiarity. Context did not collapse them; it activated governance, allegiance, recurrent counter-speech, causal repair, social permeability, and branch-distant models of trembling lift, refused exchange, and spark-like circulation.","remaining_uncertainties":["The caregiving model is branch-backed but may be an affective analogy rather than a governing sense of the phrase.","The countervoice model infers interruption from acoustic contrast; the packet does not state that voicing causally silences whispering.","The social-carrier reading follows the final source pair but cannot determine whether human and concealed agency operate identically.","The trembling-lift and exchange models depend on non-dominant split mappings and must remain explicitly exploratory."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Voiced command against inward whisper.** Evidence: ق و ل B001, uttered speech; ق و ل B012, unspoken inner saying; و س و س B001/B002 from 114:4-5, hidden inward whisper and hidden rustle; ص د ر B001 from 114:5, chest. Structural cue: “قُلْ” is the first ordinary surah word, while the later threat is a voice operating inside the chest. Assigned role: commanded utterance externalizes and orders speech before the hidden inward voice appears. Abductive move: the packet frames protection as a speech-event because the danger is also speech-like. Reading change: “Say” reads as an operative counter-mechanism, not only as instruction to recite.\n\n2. **Refuge as spoken protection and enclosure.** Evidence: ع و ذ B001, turning for refuge and protection; ع و ذ B002, protective charm or invocation; ع و ذ B004, clinging close beside something; ج ن ن B008 from 114:6, protective cover; خ ن س B001 from 114:4, withdrawal into concealment. Structural cue: the act “أعوذ” follows “قل” and precedes the description of a retreating whisperer. Assigned role: refuge is a voiced protective covering that relocates the speaker near the protector. Abductive move: the thing that makes the whisperer “خناس” is not only divine power in general but the invoked refuge that causes withdrawal. Reading change: “I seek refuge” reads as entering a shielded nearness that alters the attacker’s movement.\n\n3. **Rabb as repairer of the unstable human field.** Evidence: ر ب ب B001, lordship/ownership/authority; ر ب ب B002, repair, nurture, and completion; ر ب ب B007, staying, clinging, and lasting; ن و س B001, swaying or dangling oscillation; م ل ك B003 from 114:2 and ء ل ه B001 from 114:3. Structural cue: “رب الناس” starts a threefold sequence over the same repeated الناس. Assigned role: رب is the caretaking owner who steadies and completes a wavering population. Abductive move: because الناس recurs as the possessed/governed/worshipping object, its root-branch image of oscillation marks the condition being treated. Reading change: “Lord of people” reads as lordship over instability, not simply a possessive title over humankind.\n\n4. **The people as swaying recipients, not a static class.** Evidence: ن و س B001, swaying or dangling oscillation; ن و س B002, driving camels; ر ب ب B014, herd or gathered wild cattle; م ل ك B008 from 114:2, leading forepart in animals. Structural cue: الناس closes 114:1, 114:2, 114:3, 114:5, and 114:6. Assigned role: the repeated object is a moveable, leadable, sway-prone field. Abductive move: the repeated noun’s root inventory allows the social group to be read through motion and susceptibility. Reading change: the refuge is sought for a population that can be driven, led, and made to oscillate.","retrospective_surprises_md":"114:5 caused the major correction: “قُلْ” and “أعوذ” are not only pious preliminaries but the packet’s counter-technology to inner whisper. The later “يوسوس في صدور الناس” reactivated ق و ل B001/B012 and ع و ذ B002, making the uttered refuge an answer to unuttered inner saying. 114:4 added the khannas mechanism: خ ن س B001 lets the refuge be read as something that makes the attacker withdraw. 114:6 widened the target from a single outside enemy to concealed and human channels, so the refuge is sought from a mixed hidden/social field.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s114/full_context_control/reader_s114_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Refuge is enacted as counter-speech.** ق و ل `B001` supplies articulated utterance, while its dormant `B012` is an unspoken saying within the self; ع و ذ `B002` supplies a protective invocation or recitation. The imperative `قُلْ` places voiced speech before the later و س و س `B001` hidden talk in `صُدُورِ ٱلنَّاسِ`. The packet supplies the two speech channels; I infer commanded articulation -> separating the speaker's chosen appeal from an intrusive inner saying. `أَعُوذُ` thus reads as a performed verbal defense, not only a privately held wish for safety.\n\n2. **Refuge is close attachment to sustaining care.** ع و ذ `B001` names turning for protection, and `B004` images clinging beside or at the base of something; ر ب ب `B002` supplies repair, nurture, and completion stage by stage. The construction `أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ` directly joins shelter to the nurturing title, while 114:5 locates the threat inside the human breast. I infer close attachment to the nurturer -> preservation and repair of the penetrated interior. This changes refuge from movement toward an abstract safe place into a durable relation of dependence and care.\n\n3. **The Lord stabilizes a wavering human subject.** The fixed `ٱلنَّاسِ` has ن و س `B001`, swaying or dangling oscillation. Its fivefold recurrence across 114:1-3, 5, and 6 is framed first by ر ب ب `B002` (nurturing repair), then by م ل ك `B001` in 114:2 (strength and cohesion), and later by the approach-withdraw pattern of `ٱلْخَنَّاسِ`. The packet gives oscillation, care, cohesion, and withdrawal; I infer covert whispering -> exploitation of human wavering, and lordly care -> regained stability. `رَبِّ ٱلنَّاسِ` thereby identifies people not just as a class but as susceptible subjects requiring continuous formation.\n\n4. **The refuge relation governs both victim and human source.** ر ب ب `B001` includes ownership and obeyed authority. The same `ٱلنَّاسِ` governed in this fixed ayah reappears as the breasts entered in 114:5 and, unexpectedly, as one source class in `مِنَ ٱلْجِنَّةِ وَٱلنَّاسِ` in 114:6. The packet supplies that role reversal; I infer the repeated genitive relation -> no human transmitter lies outside the Rabb's field of authority. The appeal is therefore protection within a socially permeable domain, not flight from a wholly external population.","retrospective_surprises_md":"114:5 clarified why `قُلْ` must be voiced by locating the rival speech inside the breast. The stronger surprise came from 114:6: `ٱلنَّاسِ` changed from protected dependents here to a possible source of whispering, correcting any reading of refuge as escape from an exclusively external other.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr-11ayah/s114/full_context_control/reader_s114_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_cross_run_publication":{"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-cross-run/tr/114_ayah_findings_publication.json","protocol":"v12-cross-run-publication-v3","language":"tr","surah":114,"ayah_ref":"114:1","canonical_ayah_ref":"114:1","baseline":{"source_ref":"114:1","text":"De: İnsanların Rabbine sığınırım.","target_tokens":[["De",["114:1:1"]],["İnsanların",["114:1:4"]],["Rabbine",["114:1:3"]],["sığınırım",["114:1:2"]]]},"findings":[{"text":"“De” buyruğu, göğüste beliren gizli söze karşı seçilmiş sığınma sözünü açıkça dile getirerek korumayı fiilen başlatır.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["114:1:1","root_001272",["B001","B012"]],["114:1:2","root_001059",["B002"]]]},{"text":"Sığınmak, soyut bir güvenlik isteğinden öte, koruyup geliştiren Rabbe yakından tutunulan kalıcı bir bakım ilişkisi olarak belirginleşir.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["114:1:2","root_001059",["B001","B004"]],["114:1:3","root_000532",["B002","B007"]]]},{"text":"Rab, fısıltının yararlandığı insani yalpalamayı sürekli bakım, onarım ve olgunlaştırmayla dengeler.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["114:1:3","root_000532",["B002"]],["114:1:4","root_004965",["B001"]]]},{"text":"Son ayette insanların fısıltıyı taşıyan kaynaklar arasında da sayılması, hiçbir insan aktarıcının Rabbin yönetim ve bakım alanının dışında kalmadığını gösterir.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["114:1:3","root_000532",["B001"]],["114:1:4","root_004965",["B001"]]]}]},"butuncul_okuma_line":{"raw_line":"**114:1.** قُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ ٱلنَّاسِ — Birincil okuma: Söylemek ve insanların Rabbine sığınmak, korunmayı açık bir söz eylemi olarak başlatır. ## Your response

Write in tr. Write exactly four files at the paths below and modify nothing else. Do not return one combined response. Omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the artifact name.

- Prose: `_commentary/outputs/s114/114_1.prose.tr.md`
- Evidence surface: `_commentary/outputs/s114/114_1.evidence.tr.md`
- Findings index: `_commentary/outputs/s114/114_1.index.tr.md`
- Friction: `_commentary/outputs/s114/114_1.friction.tr.md`

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
